@theme {
    --font-sans: 'Instrument Sans', ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji',
        'Segoe UI Symbol', 'Noto Color Emoji';

    /* Logo wordmark (used by <x-logo /> via the font-logo class) */
    --font-logo: 'Outfit', ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;

    --nav-bg: var(--color-surface);
    --nav-text: var(--color-charcoal);
    --nav-link-hover: var(--color-ice-blue);
    --nav-active: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-primary) 10%, var(--color-surface));
    --nav-border: var(--color-line);
    --nav-overlay: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-charcoal) 40%, transparent);
    --z-nav: 32;
    --z-nav-overlay: 40;
    --z-nav-panel: 45;
    --z-nav-dropdown: 48;
    --z-modal: 90;

    /* Brand palette */
    --color-primary: #1A5FB4;
    --color-sky-blue: #4A90D9;
    --color-charcoal: #2E3A46;
    --color-leaf-green: #4CAF7D;
    --color-sale-red: #E4572E;
    --color-warning: #F97316;
    --color-light-gray: #F5F7FA;

    /* Page canvas: an almost-white neutral. Just enough off-white that white
       cards and their borders still read, without any blue tint. */
    --color-ice-blue: #F8FAFC;

    /* Surfaces and lines */
    --color-surface: #FFFFFF;
    --color-line: #DDE3EA;

    /* Semantic aliases: use these for UI states, not the brand names above */
    --color-success: var(--color-leaf-green);
    --color-danger: var(--color-sale-red);

    /*
    | Radius scale
    | Overriding Tailwind's defaults means every existing rounded-* class in
    | every Blade file gets squarer automatically, with no markup changes.
    |
    |   rounded-sm        4px   badges, tags
    |   rounded-md/lg   5-6px   buttons, inputs, dropdowns
    |   rounded-xl/2xl    8px   cards, panels
    |   rounded-3xl      12px   modals, large containers
    |   rounded-full            avatars, dots, switches (unchanged)
    */
    --radius-xs: 0.125rem;
    --radius-sm: 0.25rem;
    --radius-md: 0.3125rem;
    --radius-lg: 0.375rem;
    --radius-xl: 0.5rem;
    --radius-2xl: 0.5rem;
    --radius-3xl: 0.75rem;
    --radius-4xl: 1rem;
}

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Surface system (formerly "frosted")
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Class names are kept so existing pages keep working. Everything is now a
| flat, solid surface: no gradients, no blur, no translucency.
*/
@layer components {

    /* Fixed page background. Plain solid color. Safe to leave the
       <div class="frost-surface"></div> in layouts until they are cleaned up. */
    .frost-surface {
        position: fixed;
        inset: 0;
        z-index: -1;
        background: var(--color-ice-blue);
    }

    /* Standard card: dashboard cards, content panels. */
    .frost-panel {
        background: var(--color-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--color-line);
        box-shadow: 0 1px 2px color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-charcoal) 5%, transparent);
    }

    /* Chrome that floats above content: topbar, sidebar, modals, dropdowns. */
    .frost-panel-solid {
        background: var(--color-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--color-line);
        box-shadow: 0 4px 16px -4px color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-charcoal) 12%, transparent);
    }

    /* Small buttons: icon toggles, collapse controls. */
    .frost-btn {
        background: var(--color-surface);
        border: 1px solid var(--color-line);
        transition: background-color 0.15s ease, color 0.15s ease;
    }

    /* Tinted pill: badges, active chips, avatar wells. */
    .frost-chip {
        background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-primary) 8%, white);
        border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-primary) 18%, white);
    }

    /* Form fields: inputs, textareas, selects. */
    .frost-input {
        background: var(--color-surface);
        border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-charcoal) 22%, white);
        color: var(--color-charcoal);
        box-shadow: 0 1px 2px color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-charcoal) 4%, transparent);
        transition: border-color 0.15s ease, box-shadow 0.15s ease;
    }

    .frost-input::placeholder {
        color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-charcoal) 55%, transparent);
    }

    .frost-input:focus-visible {
        border-color: var(--color-primary);
        box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-primary) 15%, transparent);
        outline: none;
    }

    /* Validation error. In Blade: aria-invalid="{{ $errors->has('email') ? 'true' : 'false' }}" */

        border-color: var(--color-danger);
        box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-danger) 15%, transparent);
    }

| Status badges
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| One base class + four semantic variants. Map each model status to a
| variant in PHP (e.g. an enum method badgeVariant()), so a new status
| never needs new CSS. The dot is drawn by ::before; markup is just text.
|
| Usage:  <span class="status-badge badge--success">Approved</span>
*/

        border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
        background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--badge-color) 10%, white);
        color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--badge-color) 80%, black);
        font-size: 0.75rem;
        line-height: 1rem;
        font-weight: 500;
        text-transform: capitalize;
    }

        content: "";

import Alpine from 'alpinejs';
import { notificationBell } from './shared/notifications.js';
import { navbar, navToggle } from './shared/navigation.js';
import { voucherCard } from './pages/buyer/vouchers.js';
import { addressBook } from './shared/buyer-addresses.js';
import { checkoutPage, voucherCheckout } from './pages/buyer/checkout.js';
import { termsAgreementState } from './shared/terms-modal.js';

window.Alpine = Alpine;
Alpine.data('shopleapBell', notificationBell);
Alpine.data('navbar', navbar);
Alpine.data('navToggle', navToggle);
Alpine.data('voucherCard', voucherCard);
Alpine.data('addressBook', addressBook);
Alpine.data('checkoutPage', checkoutPage);
Alpine.data('voucherCheckout', voucherCheckout);

Alpine.data('otpRegistration', (config = {}) => ({
    ...termsAgreementState(config),

    verifyUrl: config.verifyUrl,
    resendUrl: config.resendUrl,
    loginUrl: config.loginUrl,

    init() {
        this.initTermsAgreement();
    },

    submitting: false,
    showOtpModal: false,
    otpDigits: ['', '', '', '', '', ''],
    otpError: '',
    formErrors: [],
    email: '',
    verifying: false,
    resending: false,
    resendCooldown: 0,
    resendTimer: null,

    csrfToken() {
        return document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').getAttribute('content');
    },

    async submitRegistration(event) {
        if (!this.termsAccepted) {
            this.openTermsModal();
            this.termsError =
                'Please read and agree to the Terms and Conditions and Privacy Policy before continuing.';
            return;
        }

        this.termsError = '';
        this.submitting = true;
        this.formErrors = [];

        const form = event.target;
        const formData = new FormData(form);

        try {
            const response = await fetch(form.action, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
                    'X-CSRF-TOKEN': this.csrfToken(),
                    'Accept': 'application/json',
                },
                body: formData,
            });

            const data = await response.json();

            if (response.status === 422) {
                const errors = data.errors ?? {};
                this.formErrors = Object.values(errors).flat();

                if (errors.terms_accepted?.length) {
                    this.termsError = errors.terms_accepted[0];
                    this.openTermsModal();
                }

                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
                return;
            }

            if (!response.ok) {
                this.formErrors = [data.message || 'Something went wrong. Please try again.'];
                return;
            }

            this.email = data.email;
            this.openOtpModal();
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
            this.formErrors = ['Network error. Please check your connection and try again.'];
        } finally {
            this.submitting = false;
        }
    },

    openOtpModal() {
        this.otpDigits = ['', '', '', '', '', ''];
        this.otpError = '';
        this.showOtpModal = true;
        this.startCooldown();
        this.$nextTick(() => this.$refs.otpInput0?.focus());
    },

    closeOtpModal() {
        this.showOtpModal = false;
        clearInterval(this.resendTimer);
    },

    handleOtpInput(index, event) {
        const digit = event.target.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 1);
        this.otpDigits[index] = digit;
        event.target.value = digit;

        if (digit && index < 5) {
            this.$refs['otpInput' + (index + 1)]?.focus();
        }

        if (this.otpDigits.every((d) => d !== '')) {
            this.verifyOtp();
        }
    },

    handleOtpKeydown(index, event) {
        if (event.key === 'Backspace' && !this.otpDigits[index] && index > 0) {
            this.$refs['otpInput' + (index - 1)]?.focus();
        }
    },

    handleOtpPaste(event) {
        const pasted = (event.clipboardData?.getData('text') || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 6);
        if (!pasted) return;
        event.preventDefault();

        pasted.split('').forEach((digit, i) => {
            this.otpDigits[i] = digit;
            if (this.$refs['otpInput' + i]) this.$refs['otpInput' + i].value = digit;
        });

        if (pasted.length === 6) {
            this.verifyOtp();
        } else {
            this.$refs['otpInput' + pasted.length]?.focus();
        }
    },

    async verifyOtp() {
        const code = this.otpDigits.join('');
        if (code.length !== 6 || this.verifying) return;

        this.verifying = true;
        this.otpError = '';

        try {
            const response = await fetch(this.verifyUrl, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                    'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
                    'X-CSRF-TOKEN': this.csrfToken(),
                    'Accept': 'application/json',
                },
                body: JSON.stringify({ otp: code }),
            });

            const data = await response.json();

            if (!response.ok) {
                this.otpError = data.message || data.errors?.otp?.[0] || 'Invalid code. Please try again.';
                this.otpDigits = ['', '', '', '', '', ''];
                this.$nextTick(() => this.$refs.otpInput0?.focus());
                return;
            }

            window.location.href = this.loginUrl;
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
            this.otpError = 'Network error. Please try again.';
        } finally {
            this.verifying = false;
        }
    },

    startCooldown() {
        this.resendCooldown = 60;
        clearInterval(this.resendTimer);
        this.resendTimer = setInterval(() => {
            this.resendCooldown--;
            if (this.resendCooldown <= 0) clearInterval(this.resendTimer);
        }, 1000);
    },

    async resendOtp() {
        if (this.resendCooldown > 0 || this.resending) return;

        this.resending = true;
        this.otpError = '';

        try {
            const response = await fetch(this.resendUrl, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
                    'X-CSRF-TOKEN': this.csrfToken(),
                    'Accept': 'application/json',
                },
            });

            const data = await response.json();

            if (!response.ok) {
                this.otpError = data.message || 'Could not resend code. Please try again.';
                return;
            }

            this.startCooldown();
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
            this.otpError = 'Network error. Please try again.';
        } finally {
            this.resending = false;
        }
    },
}));

Alpine.store('wishlist', {
    ids: {},          // { [productId]: true }
    count: 0,
    pending: {},      // { [productId]: true } while a request is in flight
    url: '',
    loginUrl: '/login',

    // Alpine calls init() automatically when the store is registered.
    init() {
        const el = document.getElementById('wishlistData');
        if (el) {
            const data = JSON.parse(el.textContent || '{}');
            (data.ids || []).forEach((id) => (this.ids[id] = true));
            this.count = data.count ?? 0;
            this.url = data.url;
            this.loginUrl = data.loginUrl || '/login';
        }

        // Back/forward cache can show a stale heart; refresh when restored.
        window.addEventListener('pageshow', (e) => {
            if (e.persisted) window.location.reload();
        });
    },

    has(id) {
        return !!this.ids[id];
    },

    set(id, on) {
        if (on) this.ids[id] = true;
        else delete this.ids[id];
    },

    headers(json = false) {
        return {
            Accept: 'application/json',
            'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]')?.content,
            ...(json && { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }),
        };
    },

    toast(message, error = false) {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('wishlist-toast', { detail: { message, error } }));
    },

    /** Optimistic toggle: update the UI now, roll back if the server says no. */
    async toggle(id) {
        if (this.pending[id]) return;

        const wasOn = this.has(id);
        this.pending[id] = true;
        this.set(id, !wasOn);
        this.count += wasOn ? -1 : 1;

        try {
            const res = await fetch(this.url.replace('__ID__', id), {
                method: wasOn ? 'DELETE' : 'POST',
                headers: this.headers(),
            });

            if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 419) {
                window.location.href = this.loginUrl;
                return;
            }

            const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
            if (!res.ok || data.success === false) {
                throw new Error(data.message || 'Could not update your wishlist.');
            }

            this.count = data.wishlist_count; // trust the server's count
            this.toast(data.message);
        } catch (e) {
            this.set(id, wasOn);
            this.count += wasOn ? 1 : -1;
            this.toast(e instanceof TypeError ? 'Network error. Please try again.' : e.message, true);
        } finally {
            delete this.pending[id];
        }
    },

    /** Used by the wishlist page. Calls your existing cart endpoint. */
    async addToCart(url) {
        try {
            const res = await fetch(url, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: this.headers(true),
                body: JSON.stringify({ quantity: 1 }),
            });
            const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));

            if (!res.ok || data.success === false) {
                this.toast(data.message || 'Could not add to cart.', true);
                return;
            }

            this.toast(data.message);
            const badge = document.getElementById('buyerCartBadge');
            if (badge && data.cart_count !== undefined) badge.textContent = data.cart_count;
        } catch {
            this.toast('Network error. Please try again.', true);
        }
    },
});

Alpine.store('follow', {
    ids: {},          // { [sellerId]: true }
    counts: {},       // { [sellerId]: follower count }
    total: 0,         // sellers this buyer follows
    pending: {},      // { [sellerId]: true } while a request is in flight
    followUrl: '',
    unfollowUrl: '',
    loginUrl: '/login',

    init() {
        const el = document.getElementById('sellerFollowData');
        if (!el) return;

        const data = JSON.parse(el.textContent || '{}');
        (data.ids || []).forEach((id) => (this.ids[id] = true));
        Object.entries(data.counts || {}).forEach(([id, count]) => {
            this.counts[id] = Number(count);
        });

        this.total = data.total ?? 0;
        this.followUrl = data.followUrl;
        this.unfollowUrl = data.unfollowUrl;
        this.loginUrl = data.loginUrl || '/login';
    },

    has(id) {
        return !!this.ids[id];
    },

    followerCount(id) {
        return this.counts[id] ?? 0;
    },

    /** Seed a seller's state on pages that show a single shop. */
    prime(id, count, following) {
        if (this.pending[id]) return;

        if (following) this.ids[id] = true;
        else delete this.ids[id];

        if (count !== null && count !== undefined) this.counts[id] = Number(count);
    },

    set(id, following) {
        if (following) this.ids[id] = true;
        else delete this.ids[id];
    },

    headers() {
        return {
            Accept: 'application/json',
            'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]')?.content,
        };
    },

    toast(message, error = false) {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('seller-follow-toast', { detail: { message, error } }));
    },

    /** Optimistic toggle: update the UI now, roll back if the server says no. */
    async toggle(id) {
        if (this.pending[id]) return;

        const wasFollowing = this.has(id);
        const previousCount = this.followerCount(id);
        const previousTotal = this.total;

        this.pending[id] = true;
        this.set(id, !wasFollowing);
        this.counts[id] = Math.max(0, previousCount + (wasFollowing ? -1 : 1));
        this.total = Math.max(0, previousTotal + (wasFollowing ? -1 : 1));

        try {
            const res = await fetch((wasFollowing ? this.unfollowUrl : this.followUrl).replace('__ID__', id), {
                method: wasFollowing ? 'DELETE' : 'POST',
                headers: this.headers(),
            });

            if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 419) {
                window.location.href = this.loginUrl;
                return;
            }

            const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
            if (!res.ok || data.success === false) {
                throw new Error(data.message || 'Could not update your following list.');
            }

            this.set(id, data.following);
            this.counts[id] = data.followers_count;
            this.total = data.following_count;
            this.toast(data.message);
        } catch (e) {
            this.set(id, wasFollowing);
            this.counts[id] = previousCount;
            this.total = previousTotal;
            this.toast(e instanceof TypeError ? 'Network error. Please try again.' : e.message, true);
        } finally {
            delete this.pending[id];
        }
    },
});

Alpine.start();

function initSearchSuggestions() {
    const wrapper = document.getElementById('searchWrapper');
    const input = document.getElementById('searchInput');
    const suggestions = document.getElementById('searchSuggestions');
    if (!wrapper || !input || !suggestions) return;

    input.addEventListener('focus', () => suggestions.classList.remove('hidden'));
    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        if (!wrapper.contains(e.target)) suggestions.classList.add('hidden');
    });

    // Clicking a trending term fills the box and searches it
    suggestions.querySelectorAll('[data-trend]').forEach((chip) => {
        chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
            input.value = chip.dataset.trend;
            input.form?.submit();
        });
    });
}

function initHeroCarousel() {
    const root = document.getElementById('heroCarousel');
    if (!root) return;

    const slides = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('.hero-slide'));
    const dots = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('.hero-dot'));
    if (slides.length < 2) return;

    let index = 0;
    let timer;

    const show = (i) => {
        slides[index].classList.remove('is-active');
        dots[index].classList.remove('is-active');
        index = (i + slides.length) % slides.length;
        slides[index].classList.add('is-active');
        dots[index].classList.add('is-active');
    };

    const start = () => {
        clearInterval(timer);
        timer = setInterval(() => show(index + 1), 5000);
    };

    dots.forEach((dot, i) => dot.addEventListener('click', () => {
        show(i);
        start();
    }));

    root.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => clearInterval(timer));
    root.addEventListener('mouseleave', start);
    start();
}

function initScrollReveal() {
    const targets = document.querySelectorAll('.reveal, .reveal-section');
    const reduceMotion = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

    if (reduceMotion || !('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
        targets.forEach((el) => el.classList.add('is-visible'));
        return;
    }

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        entries.forEach((entry, i) => {
            if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
            entry.target.style.transitionDelay = `${(i % 6) * 60}ms`;
            entry.target.classList.add('is-visible');
            observer.unobserve(entry.target);
        });
    }, { threshold: 0, rootMargin: '150px 0px 150px 0px' });

    targets.forEach((el) => observer.observe(el));
}

/**
 * Images marked data-fallback="hide" are removed if they fail to load,
 * so the coloured background behind them shows instead of a broken icon.
 * data-fallback="<url>" swaps in that image once.
 */
function initImageFallbacks() {
    const handle = (img) => {
        const fallback = img.dataset.fallback;
        if (!fallback || img.dataset.fallbackDone) return;
        img.dataset.fallbackDone = '1';

        if (fallback === 'hide') {
            img.style.display = 'none';
        } else {
            img.src = fallback;
        }
    };

    // "error" doesn't bubble, so listen in the capture phase
    document.addEventListener('error', (e) => {
        if (e.target instanceof HTMLImageElement) handle(e.target);
    }, true);

    // Catch images that failed before this script ran
    document.querySelectorAll('img[data-fallback]').forEach((img) => {
        if (img.complete && img.naturalWidth === 0) handle(img);
    });
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    initSearchSuggestions();
    initHeroCarousel();
    initScrollReveal();
    initImageFallbacks();
});

const locks = new Set();
let previousOverflow = '';
let previousBodyOverflow = '';

function scrollLock(id, enabled) {
    if (!enabled && !locks.has(id)) { return; }
    if (enabled && !locks.size) {
        previousOverflow = document.documentElement.style.overflow;
        previousBodyOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    }
    if (enabled) { locks.add(id); } else { locks.delete(id); }
    document.documentElement.style.overflow = locks.size ? 'hidden' : previousOverflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = locks.size ? 'hidden' : previousBodyOverflow;
}

export function navbar(config = {}) {
    return {
        id: config.id, desktop: window.innerWidth >= 1024, open: false, collapsed: false, activeDropdown: null, logoutOpen: false,
        logoutForm: null, returnFocus: null, dropdownTrigger: null, unread: 0, poll: null,
        init() {
            try { this.collapsed = localStorage.getItem(`navigation:${this.id}:collapsed`) === 'true'; } catch {}
            this.$watch('open', () => this.syncLock());
            this.$watch('logoutOpen', () => this.syncLock());
            if (config.unreadUrl) {
                this.refreshUnread();
                this.poll = setInterval(() => this.refreshUnread(), 20000);
            }
        },
        destroy() { clearInterval(this.poll); scrollLock(this.id, false); },
        syncLock() { scrollLock(this.id, this.logoutOpen || (this.open && window.innerWidth < 1024)); },
        toggle(trigger = null) {
            if (window.innerWidth >= 1024) { return; }
            if (this.open) { this.close(); return; }
            this.returnFocus = trigger || document.activeElement;
            this.activeDropdown = null; this.open = true;
            this.broadcast();
            this.$nextTick(() => this.$refs.panel?.querySelector('button, a[href], input')?.focus());
        },
        close(restoreFocus = true) {
            const wasOpen = this.open;
            this.open = false; this.activeDropdown = null; this.syncLock(); this.broadcast();
            if (wasOpen && restoreFocus) { this.returnFocus?.focus(); }
        },
        broadcast() { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('navigation-state', { detail: { id: this.id, open: this.open } })); },
        resize() { this.desktop = window.innerWidth >= 1024; if (this.desktop) { this.close(false); } },
        collapse() {
            this.collapsed = !this.collapsed;
            try { localStorage.setItem(`navigation:${this.id}:collapsed`, String(this.collapsed)); } catch {}
        },
        navigate(event) { if (event.target.closest('a[href]')) { this.close(false); } },
        escape() {
            if (this.logoutOpen) { this.cancelLogout(); return; }
            if (this.activeDropdown) { this.activeDropdown = null; this.dropdownTrigger?.focus(); return; }
            this.close();
        },
        dropdown(id, trigger) {
            this.dropdownTrigger = trigger;
            this.activeDropdown = this.activeDropdown === id ? null : id;
        },
        focusMenu(id, trigger) {
            this.dropdownTrigger = trigger; this.activeDropdown = id;
            this.$nextTick(() => trigger.closest('[data-nav-dropdown]')?.querySelector('[data-nav-dropdown-menu] a, [data-nav-dropdown-menu] button')?.focus());
        },
        moveMenu(event, direction) {
            const root = event.target.closest('[data-nav-dropdown-menu]');
            if (!root) { return; }
            const items = [...root.querySelectorAll('a[href], button:not([disabled])')];
            if (!items.length) { return; }
            event.preventDefault(); items[(items.indexOf(document.activeElement) + direction + items.length) % items.length]?.focus();
        },
        trap(event) {
            if (event.key !== 'Tab' || (!this.open && !this.logoutOpen)) { return; }
            const root = this.logoutOpen ? this.$refs.logoutDialog : this.$refs.panel;
            const items = [...(root?.querySelectorAll('a[href], button:not([disabled]), input, select, textarea') || [])].filter(el => el.getClientRects().length);
            if (!items.length) { return; }
            const first = items[0]; const last = items.at(-1);
            if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) { event.preventDefault(); last.focus(); }
            else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) { event.preventDefault(); first.focus(); }
        },
        requestLogout(form) {
            this.logoutForm = form; this.close(false); this.logoutOpen = true;
            this.$nextTick(() => this.$refs.logoutDialog?.querySelector('button')?.focus());
        },
        cancelLogout() {
            this.logoutOpen = false;
            if (window.innerWidth < 1024) { this.returnFocus?.focus(); }
            else { this.dropdownTrigger?.focus(); }
        },
        confirmLogout() { this.logoutForm?.submit(); },
        async refreshUnread() {
            if (document.hidden) { return; }
            try {
                const response = await fetch(config.unreadUrl, { headers: { Accept: 'application/json' } });
                if (response.ok) { this.unread = Number((await response.json()).count) || 0; }
            } catch { /* Retain the last known count during a temporary network failure. */ }
        },
    };
}

export function navToggle(id) {
    return { expanded: false, toggle() { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('navigation-toggle', { detail: { id, trigger: this.$el } })); } };
}

export function notificationBell(cfg) {
            return {
                open: false,
                loading: false,
                failed: false,
                unread: cfg.unread,
                items: [],

                init() {
                    if (cfg.navDropdownId) {
                        this.$watch('activeDropdown', value => {
                            if (value === cfg.navDropdownId) {
                                this.loading = true;
                                this.refresh().finally(() => this.loading = false);
                            }
                        });
                    }
                    // Keep the count and list fresh while the tab is visible.
                    setInterval(() => {
                        if (!document.hidden) this.refresh();
                    }, 45000);

                    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
                        if (!document.hidden) this.refresh();
                    });
                },

                badge() {
                    return this.unread > 99 ? '99+' : this.unread;
                },

                async refresh() {
                    try {
                        const res = await fetch(cfg.feedUrl, {
                            headers: {
                                'Accept': 'application/json'
                            },
                            credentials: 'same-origin',
                        });

                        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('feed ' + res.status);

                        const data = await res.json();
                        this.items = data.items;
                        this.unread = data.unread;
                        this.failed = false;
                    } catch (e) {
                        this.failed = true;
                    }
                },

                toggle() {
                    this.open = !this.open;

                    if (this.open) {
                        this.loading = true;
                        this.refresh().finally(() => this.loading = false);
                    }
                },

                // Mark it read, then go to the page it is about.
                async go(item) {
                    try {
                        await fetch(cfg.readUrl.replace('__ID__', item.id), {
                            method: 'POST',
                            headers: {
                                'X-CSRF-TOKEN': cfg.csrf,
                                'Accept': 'application/json'
                            },
                            credentials: 'same-origin',
                        });
                    } catch (e) {
                        // Still navigate. It will be marked read the next time it is opened.
                    }

                    window.location.href = item.url || cfg.allUrl;
                },

                async markAll() {
                    try {
                        const res = await fetch(cfg.readAllUrl, {
                            method: 'POST',
                            headers: {
                                'X-CSRF-TOKEN': cfg.csrf,
                                'Accept': 'application/json'
                            },
                            credentials: 'same-origin',
                        });

                        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('read-all ' + res.status);

                        this.items = this.items.map(i => ({
                            ...i,
                            read: true
                        }));
                        this.unread = 0;
                    } catch (e) {
                        this.failed = true;
                    }
                },
            };

}

                                                {{ $log->category }}

FILE: resources/views/auth/password-recovery.blade.php

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Reset password | Shopleap</title>
    @vite(['resources/css/app.css', 'resources/js/app.js', 'resources/js/pages/auth/password-recovery.js'])
</head>
<body class="min-h-dvh bg-ice-blue text-charcoal">
<x-nav.guest />
<main class="mx-auto flex min-h-dvh max-w-lg items-center px-4 py-10">
    <section class="w-full rounded-md border border-charcoal/10 bg-white p-6 sm:p-8">
        <a href="{{ route('login') }}" class="font-bold text-primary">Shopleap</a>
        <h1 class="mt-6 text-2xl font-bold">Reset your password</h1>
        <ol class="my-6 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-xs" aria-label="Password recovery progress">
            @foreach ([1 => 'Email', 2 => 'Verify OTP', 3 => 'New password'] as $number => $label)
                <li class="border-t-4 pt-2 {{ $step >= $number ? 'border-primary text-primary' : 'border-charcoal/15' }}" @if ($step === $number) aria-current="step" @endif>{{ $number }}. {{ $label }}</li>
            @endforeach
        </ol>
        @if (session('status'))
            <p role="status" class="mb-4 rounded border border-primary/20 bg-ice-blue p-3 text-sm">{{ session('status') }}</p>
        @endif
        @if ($step === 1)
            <p class="mb-5 text-sm">Enter the email you use to log in.</p>
            <form method="POST" action="{{ route('password.email') }}" data-recovery-form class="space-y-4">
                @csrf
                <label for="email" class="block text-sm font-semibold">Email address</label>
                <input id="email" name="email" type="email" autocomplete="email" required maxlength="255" value="{{ old('email') }}" class="w-full rounded border border-charcoal/25 px-3 py-3">
                @error('email') <p role="alert" class="text-sm text-sale-red">{{ $message }}</p> @enderror
                <button class="btn btn-primary w-full" data-loading="Sending?">Send OTP</button>
            </form>
        @elseif ($step === 2)
            <p class="mb-5 text-sm">Enter the six-digit code sent to {{ $maskedEmail }}. It expires in 10 minutes.</p>
            <form method="POST" action="{{ route('password.verify.store') }}" data-recovery-form class="space-y-4">
                @csrf
                <label for="otp" class="block text-sm font-semibold">Verification code</label>
                <input id="otp" name="otp" required pattern="[0-9]{6}" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="one-time-code" maxlength="6" class="w-full rounded border border-charcoal/25 px-3 py-3" data-otp-value>
                <div class="hidden grid-cols-6 gap-2" data-otp-boxes>
                    @for ($digit = 0; $digit < 6; $digit++)
                        <input type="text" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="{{ $digit === 0 ? 'one-time-code' : 'off' }}" maxlength="6" aria-label="Digit {{ $digit + 1 }}" data-otp-digit class="min-w-0 rounded border border-charcoal/25 py-3 text-center text-xl focus:border-primary focus:outline-primary">
                    @endfor
                </div>
                @error('otp') <p role="alert" class="text-sm text-sale-red">{{ $message }}</p> @enderror
                <button class="btn btn-primary w-full" data-loading="Verifying?">Verify OTP</button>
            </form>
            <form method="POST" action="{{ route('password.resend') }}" data-recovery-form class="mt-4">
                @csrf
                <button class="btn btn-secondary w-full" data-resend data-cooldown="{{ $cooldown }}" data-loading="Sending?">Resend OTP</button>
                @error('email') <p role="alert" class="mt-2 text-sm text-sale-red">{{ $message }}</p> @enderror
            </form>
            <a href="{{ route('password.request') }}" class="mt-4 block text-sm text-primary">Use a different email</a>
        @else
            <p class="mb-5 text-sm">Choose a new password of at least eight characters.</p>
            <form method="POST" action="{{ route('password.update') }}" data-recovery-form class="space-y-4">
                @csrf
                <label for="password" class="block text-sm font-semibold">New password</label>
                <input id="password" name="password" type="password" required minlength="8" autocomplete="new-password" class="w-full rounded border border-charcoal/25 px-3 py-3">
                @error('password') <p role="alert" class="text-sm text-sale-red">{{ $message }}</p> @enderror
                <label for="password_confirmation" class="block text-sm font-semibold">Confirm password</label>
                <input id="password_confirmation" name="password_confirmation" type="password" required minlength="8" autocomplete="new-password" class="w-full rounded border border-charcoal/25 px-3 py-3">
                <button class="btn btn-primary w-full" data-loading="Saving?">Reset password</button>
            </form>
        @endif
        <a href="{{ route('login') }}" class="mt-6 block text-center text-sm font-semibold text-primary">Back to login</a>
    </section>
</main>
</body>
</html>

@php
    $headerUser = auth()->user();

    // Compute cart data only when the page didn't pass it in.
    if ($cartCount === null || $cartPreviewItems === null) {
        $cartPreviewItems = $headerUser
            ? $headerUser->cartItems()->with('product')->latest()->take(5)->get()
            : collect();
        $cartCount = $headerUser ? $headerUser->cartItems()->count() : 0;
    }

{{-- WISHLIST / FOLLOW: shared toast for page-level action feedback --}}
<div x-data="{
    show: false,
    message: '',
    error: false,
    timer: null,
    notify(detail) {
        this.message = detail.message;
        this.error = detail.error;
        this.show = true;
        clearTimeout(this.timer);
        this.timer = setTimeout(() => (this.show = false), 3000);
    },
}" @wishlist-toast.window="notify($event.detail)"
    @seller-follow-toast.window="notify($event.detail)" x-show="show" x-cloak x-transition role="status"
    aria-live="polite" :class="error ? 'bg-sale-red' : 'bg-charcoal'"
    class="fixed bottom-20 right-3 z-[80] max-w-[calc(100vw-1.5rem)] rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white shadow-lg sm:bottom-6 sm:right-6 sm:max-w-sm">
    <span x-text="message"></span>
</div>

                <div class="mt-5 flex gap-3"><button type="button" class="btn btn-outline flex-1" @click="cancelLogout()">Cancel</button><button type="button" class="btn btn-danger flex-1" @click="confirmLogout()">Log out</button></div>

                    $selected = request()->routeIs($link['route'] === 'admin.settings.index' ? $link['route'] : $pattern);

                @endphp

            @endforeach
        @endforeach

        <x-nav.user-menu id="workspace-user-menu" :items="[['route' => 'notifications.index', 'label' => 'Notifications']]" />
    </x-slot:actions>

@props(['variant' => 'frost']) {{-- 'frost' for the seller/admin/logistics headers, 'primary' for the blue buyer header --}}

@auth
    @php
        $isPrimary = $variant === 'primary';

        $bellButton = $isPrimary
            ? 'relative flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-white transition hover:bg-white/10 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-white/40'
            : 'frost-btn relative flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-charcoal/70 transition hover:text-primary focus:outline-none focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary/10';

        $badgeRing = $isPrimary ? 'ring-primary' : 'ring-white';
    @endphp



    <div class="relative shrink-0" @keydown.escape.window="open = false" x-data="shopleapBell({
        unread: {{ (int) auth()->user()->unreadNotifications()->count() }},
        feedUrl: @js(route('notifications.feed', [], false)),
        readUrl: @js(route('notifications.read', ['id' => '__ID__'], false)),
        readAllUrl: @js(route('notifications.read-all', [], false)),
        allUrl: @js(route('notifications.index', [], false)),
        csrf: @js(csrf_token()),
    })">

        <button type="button" @click="toggle()" :aria-expanded="open.toString()" aria-haspopup="true"
            aria-label="Notifications" class="{{ $bellButton }}">
            <svg class="h-6 w-6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2"
                    d="M15 17h5l-1.4-1.4A2 2 0 0118 14.2V11a6 6 0 00-4-5.7V5a2 2 0 00-4 0v.3A6 6 0 006 11v3.2a2 2 0 01-.6 1.4L4 17h5m2 0v1a2 2 0 004 0v-1" />
            </svg>

            <span x-show="unread > 0" x-cloak x-text="badge()"
                class="absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 flex h-5 min-w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-sale-red px-1 text-[11px] font-bold leading-none text-white ring-2 {{ $badgeRing }}"></span>
        </button>

        <div x-show="open" x-cloak x-transition.opacity.duration.150ms @click.outside="open = false"
            class="fixed inset-x-3 top-[4.5rem] z-[70] overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-light-gray bg-white text-charcoal shadow-xl shadow-charcoal/10 sm:absolute sm:inset-x-auto sm:right-0 sm:top-full sm:mt-2 sm:w-96">

            {{-- Header --}}
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-light-gray px-4 py-3">
                <p class="text-sm font-bold text-charcoal">Notifications</p>

                <button type="button" x-show="unread > 0" x-cloak @click="markAll()"
                    class="text-xs font-semibold text-primary hover:underline">
                    Mark all as read
                </button>
            </div>

            {{-- List --}}
            <div class="max-h-[26rem] divide-y divide-light-gray overflow-y-auto">
                <p x-show="loading && !items.length" class="px-4 py-8 text-center text-xs text-charcoal/50">
                    Loading…
                </p>

                <div x-show="failed && !items.length" x-cloak class="px-4 py-8 text-center">
                    <p class="text-xs text-charcoal/50">Couldn't load your notifications.</p>
                    <button type="button" @click="refresh()"
                        class="mt-2 text-xs font-semibold text-primary hover:underline">
                        Try again
                    </button>
                </div>

                <div x-show="!loading && !failed && !items.length" x-cloak class="px-4 py-10 text-center">
                    <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-charcoal">You're all caught up</p>
                    <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-charcoal/50">Updates about your orders appear here.</p>
                </div>

                <template x-for="item in items" :key="item.id">
                    <button type="button" @click="go(item)"
                        class="flex w-full items-start gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-left transition hover:bg-ice-blue/60"
                        :class="item.read ? '' : 'bg-primary/5'">
                        <span class="mt-1.5 h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full"
                            :class="item.read ? 'bg-transparent' : 'bg-primary'"></span>

                        <span class="min-w-0 flex-1">
                            <span class="block text-sm"
                                :class="item.read ? 'font-semibold text-charcoal/80' : 'font-bold text-charcoal'"
                                x-text="item.title"></span>
                            <span class="mt-0.5 line-clamp-2 block text-xs text-charcoal/60" x-text="item.body"></span>
                            <span class="mt-1 block text-[11px] text-charcoal/40" x-text="item.time"></span>
                        </span>
                    </button>
                </template>
            </div>

            {{-- Footer --}}
            <a href="{{ route('notifications.index') }}"
                class="block border-t border-light-gray px-4 py-3 text-center text-xs font-semibold text-primary transition hover:bg-ice-blue/60">
                View all notifications
            </a>
        </div>
    </div>
@endauth

    @php
        $categories = config('landing.categories');
        $logo = asset('images/shopleap-logo.png');

        $riderRegisterUrl = Route::has('register.rider')
            ? route('register.rider')
            : (Route::has('register.courier')
                ? route('register.courier')
                : route('register'));

        // A slide/CTA target is a route name, "rider", or a "#anchor".
        $resolveTarget = fn(string $target) => match (true) {
            $target === 'rider' => $riderRegisterUrl,
            str_starts_with($target, '#') => $target,
            default => route($target),
        };
    @endphp

    {{-- ================= HEADER ================= --}}

                    @foreach (config('landing.slides') as $i => $slide)
                        <div class="hero-slide @if ($i === 0) is-active @endif">
                            <div class="grid items-center gap-8 lg:grid-cols-2 lg:gap-12">
                                <div class="max-w-xl">
                                    <span
                                        class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-white/10 px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white">

                                        <a href="{{ $resolveTarget($slide['cta_target']) }}"

                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    @endforeach
                </div>

@php
    // Mobile: each row becomes a card, each cell shows its label (from data-label).
    // md+: normal table. Labels are CSS pseudo-elements, so no extra markup is needed.
    $cell =
        'flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-1 py-1.5 before:text-xs before:font-medium before:text-charcoal/50 before:content-[attr(data-label)] md:table-cell md:px-5 md:py-4 md:before:hidden';
@endphp
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">

<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">

    @vite(['resources/css/app.css', 'resources/js/app.js'])

    <title>Orders | Shopleap Seller</title>
</head>

<body class="min-h-screen overflow-x-hidden text-charcoal">

    <div class="frost-surface pointer-events-none" aria-hidden="true"></div>

    <div class="flex min-h-screen">

        <x-navbars.seller :active="request('status') === 'prepare' ? 'prepare' : 'orders'" />

        <div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">

            <header
                class="frost-panel-solid sticky top-0 z-10 flex h-16 items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-white/50 px-4 sm:h-20 sm:px-6">
                <div class="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
                    <x-nav.toggle target="workspace-navigation" />

                    <div class="min-w-0">
                        <h2 class="truncate text-base font-bold text-charcoal sm:text-xl">Orders</h2>
                        <p class="truncate text-xs text-charcoal/55 sm:text-sm">{{ $orders->total() }} order(s) total
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-4">
                    <x-notification-bell />
                </div>
            </header>

            <main class="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-4 p-4 sm:space-y-5 sm:p-6">

                {{-- CANCELLATION REQUEST NOTICE --}}
                @if (($pendingCancelRequests ?? 0) > 0 && ($status ?? 'all') !== 'cancel_requests')
                    <a href="{{ route('seller.orders.index', ['status' => 'cancel_requests']) }}"
                        class="frost-panel flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-warning/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-charcoal transition hover:bg-white/60">
                        <span>
                            <b>{{ $pendingCancelRequests }}</b> cancellation request(s) waiting for your response.
                        </span>
                        <span class="shrink-0 text-xs font-semibold text-primary">View</span>
                    </a>
                @endif

                <!-- SEARCH & FILTER -->
                <form method="GET" role="search"
                    class="frost-panel grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:flex sm:items-center">

                    <div class="relative col-span-2 sm:flex-1">
                        <div class="pointer-events-none absolute inset-y-0 left-0 flex items-center pl-3.5">
                            <svg class="h-4 w-4 text-charcoal/40" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"
                                viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.8"
                                    d="M21 21l-4.35-4.35M17 10a7 7 0 11-14 0 7 7 0 0114 0z" />
                            </svg>
                        </div>

                        <input type="search" name="search" value="{{ request('search') }}"
                            placeholder="Search by order # or buyer name..." aria-label="Search orders"
                            class="frost-input w-full rounded-xl py-2.5 pl-10 pr-4 text-base outline-none transition focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary/10 sm:text-sm">
                    </div>

                    <select name="status" onchange="this.form.submit()" aria-label="Filter by status"
                        class="frost-input min-w-0 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-base outline-none transition focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary/10 sm:w-auto sm:text-sm">

                        <option value="all" {{ request('status', 'all') === 'all' ? 'selected' : '' }}>
                            All Status
                        </option>

                        {{-- CANCELLATION REQUEST FILTER --}}
                        <option value="cancel_requests" {{ request('status') === 'cancel_requests' ? 'selected' : '' }}>
                            Cancellation requests
                        </option>

                        @foreach (\App\Models\Order::STATUS_LABELS as $value => $label)
                            <option value="{{ $value }}" {{ request('status') === $value ? 'selected' : '' }}>
                                {{ $label }}
                            </option>
                        @endforeach
                    </select>

                    <button type="submit"
                        class="rounded-xl bg-primary px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-sky-blue">
                        Search
                    </button>
                </form>

                <!-- ORDER LIST (cards on mobile, table from md) -->
                <div class="frost-panel-solid overflow-hidden rounded-2xl">

                    @if ($orders->isEmpty())

                        <div class="flex h-64 flex-col items-center justify-center px-4 text-center">
                            <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-charcoal">No orders found</p>
                            <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-charcoal/50">
                                New orders from buyers will appear here.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    @else
                        <div class="overflow-x-auto">
                            <table class="block w-full text-left text-sm md:table">
                                <thead class="hidden border-b border-white/50 bg-primary/5 md:table-header-group">
                                    <tr>
                                        <th scope="col" class="px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-charcoal/60">Order</th>
                                        <th scope="col" class="px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-charcoal/60">Buyer</th>
                                        <th scope="col" class="px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-charcoal/60">Items</th>
                                        <th scope="col" class="px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-charcoal/60">Total</th>
                                        <th scope="col" class="px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-charcoal/60">Status</th>
                                        <th scope="col" class="px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-charcoal/60">Placed</th>
                                        <th scope="col" class="px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-charcoal/60"><span
                                                class="sr-only">Actions</span></th>
                                    </tr>
                                </thead>

                                {{-- Divider: on md+ every cell of every row except the last gets a bottom line.
                                     Applied on cells so it works with any border-collapse mode.
                                     Replaces the old divide-y, which is unreliable on table-row-group.
                                     Mobile rows are already separate bordered cards. --}}
                                <tbody
                                    class="block space-y-3 p-3 md:table-row-group md:space-y-0 md:p-0 md:[&>tr:not(:last-child)>td]:border-b md:[&>tr:not(:last-child)>td]:border-charcoal/10">
                                    @foreach ($orders as $order)
                                        <tr
                                            class="block rounded-xl border border-white/50 bg-white/30 p-3 transition hover:bg-white/40 md:table-row md:rounded-none md:border-0 md:bg-transparent md:p-0">

                                            <td
                                                class="block px-1 pb-1 text-base font-semibold text-charcoal md:table-cell md:px-5 md:py-4 md:text-sm">
                                                #{{ $order->id }}
                                            </td>

                                            <td data-label="Buyer" class="{{ $cell }} text-charcoal/80">
                                                <span class="min-w-0 truncate">{{ $order->buyer_name }}</span>
                                            </td>

                                            <td data-label="Items" class="{{ $cell }} text-charcoal/70">
                                                {{ $order->items->sum('quantity') }} item(s)
                                            </td>

                                            <td data-label="Total"
                                                class="{{ $cell }} font-semibold text-charcoal">
                                                ₱{{ number_format($order->total_amount, 2) }}
                                            </td>

                                            <td data-label="Status" class="{{ $cell }}">
                                                <div
                                                    class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-1 md:justify-start">
                                                    <span
                                                        class="rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold {{ $order->status_color }}">
                                                        {{ $order->status_label }}
                                                    </span>

                                                    {{-- PENDING CANCELLATION REQUEST CHIP --}}
                                                    @if (
                                                        $order->cancel_request_status === 'pending' &&
                                                            in_array($order->status, \App\Models\Order::BUYER_CANCEL_REQUEST_FROM, true))
                                                        <span
                                                            class="inline-block rounded-full bg-warning/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-warning">
                                                            Cancel requested
                                                        </span>
                                                    @endif
                                                </div>
                                            </td>

                                            <td data-label="Placed" class="{{ $cell }} text-charcoal/60">
                                                {{ $order->created_at->diffForHumans() }}
                                            </td>

                                            <td class="block pt-2 md:table-cell md:px-5 md:py-4 md:pt-4 md:text-right">
                                                <a href="{{ route('seller.orders.show', $order) }}"
                                                    class="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-primary px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-sky-blue md:inline-flex md:w-auto md:py-2">
                                                    View
                                                </a>
                                            </td>

                                        </tr>
                                    @endforeach
                                </tbody>
                            </table>
                        </div>

                    @endif

                </div>

                <div class="overflow-x-auto">{{ $orders->links() }}</div>

            </main>

        </div>

    </div>

</body>

</html>

                            </div>

                                Weight per unit. Used for delivery and logistics. Variants can override it below.

                                + Add Option
                            </button>

                        <div id="optionsContainer" class="space-y-3"></div>

                        <button type="button" id="generateVariantsBtn"
                            class="mt-4 hidden w-full rounded-xl border border-dashed border-primary/30 bg-primary/5 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-primary transition hover:bg-primary/10">
                            Generate Variant Combinations

                        <p class="mb-1 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-primary">Product Photos</p>
                        <p class="mb-4 text-xs text-charcoal/50">Upload up to 5 images for the whole product. The first
                            image is the main thumbnail. If you give each variant its own photo, general photos are
                            optional.
                        </p>

                        <label for="images"
                            class="group flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-light-gray bg-ice-blue px-6 py-8 text-center transition hover:border-primary/40 hover:bg-ice-blue/60">
                            <div
                                class="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-primary shadow-sm transition group-hover:bg-primary group-hover:text-white">
                                <svg class="h-6 w-6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">

                            <input type="file" id="images" name="images[]" multiple
                                accept=".jpg,.jpeg,.png,.webp" class="sr-only">
                        </label>

                        <div id="imagePreviewGrid" class="mt-4 grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-6"></div>
