                            You can upload up to 5 product images. Extra files were ignored.
                        </p>

                        <p id="imageRequirementNote" class="mt-2 hidden text-xs font-semibold text-sale-red">
                            Add at least one product photo, or upload a photo for at least one variant, before
                            publishing.
                        </p>
                    </section>

                    <div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                        <button type="submit" name="status" value="draft" formnovalidate
                            class="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-primary/40 bg-white px-6 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-primary shadow-sm transition hover:bg-primary/5">
                            Save as Draft
                        </button>

                        <button type="submit" name="status" value="active"
                            class="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-primary px-6 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-lg shadow-seller/20 transition hover:bg-sky-blue hover:shadow-xl">
                            Publish Product
                            <svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2"
                                    d="M12 4v16m8-8H4" />
                            </svg>
                        </button>
                    </div>

                </form>

            </main>

        </div>

    </div>

    <script>
        const esc = (s) => String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({
            '&': '&amp;',
            '<': '&lt;',
            '>': '&gt;',
            '"': '&quot;',
            "'": '&#39;'
        } [c]));

        const MAX_OPTION_GROUPS = 3;
        const inputCls =
            'w-full rounded-lg border border-light-gray px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-primary focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary/10';

        // ---------------------------------------------------
        // OPTION GROUPS (e.g. "Pack Size" -> "Pack of 4, Pack of 5")
        // ---------------------------------------------------
        let optionRowCount = 0;
        const optionsContainer = document.getElementById('optionsContainer');
        const addOptionBtn = document.getElementById('addOptionBtn');
        const generateVariantsBtn = document.getElementById('generateVariantsBtn');
        const variantsWrapper = document.getElementById('variantsWrapper');
        const variantsBody = document.getElementById('variantsBody');
        const basePricingFields = document.getElementById('basePricingFields');
        const pricingHint = document.getElementById('pricingHint');
        const basePrice = document.getElementById('basePrice');
        const baseStock = document.getElementById('baseStock');

        // Remembers previously entered price/stock/sku per combination key
        // so regenerating variants doesn't wipe out what was typed.
        const savedVariantData = new Map();

        function addOptionRow(name = '', values = '') {
            const index = optionRowCount++;
            const row = document.createElement('div');
            row.className = 'flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-light-gray p-3';
            row.dataset.rowIndex = index;
            row.innerHTML = `
                <div class="flex-1">
                    <input type="text" name="options[${index}][name]" value="${esc(name)}" maxlength="100"
                        placeholder="Option name (e.g. Pack Size)" class="option-name-input ${inputCls}">
                </div>
                <div class="flex-[2]">
                    <input type="text" name="options[${index}][values]" value="${esc(values)}" maxlength="500"
                        placeholder="Values, comma-separated (e.g. Pack of 4, Pack of 5)" class="option-values-input ${inputCls}">
                </div>
                <button type="button" aria-label="Remove option" class="remove-option-btn mt-1 shrink-0 rounded-lg p-2 text-charcoal/40 transition hover:bg-sale-red/10 hover:text-sale-red">
                    <svg class="h-4 w-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12" />
                    </svg>
                </button>
            `;
            row.querySelector('.remove-option-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
                row.remove();
                refreshOptionState();
            });
            row.querySelectorAll('input').forEach(input => {
                input.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    // [ and ] would break the variants[i][options][<name>] field names.
                    if (input.classList.contains('option-name-input')) {
                        input.value = input.value.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '');
                    }
                    refreshOptionState();
                });
            });
            optionsContainer.appendChild(row);
            refreshOptionState();
        }

        function getCurrentOptionGroups() {
            return Array.from(optionsContainer.children)
                .map(row => ({
                    name: row.querySelector('.option-name-input').value.trim(),
                    values: [...new Set(
                        row.querySelector('.option-values-input').value
                        .split(',')
                        .map(v => v.trim())
                        .filter(Boolean)
                    )],
                }))
                .filter(group => group.name && group.values.length > 0);
        }

        function refreshOptionState() {
            const groups = getCurrentOptionGroups();
            const atLimit = optionsContainer.children.length >= MAX_OPTION_GROUPS;

            addOptionBtn.disabled = atLimit;
            addOptionBtn.classList.toggle('opacity-50', atLimit);
            generateVariantsBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', groups.length === 0);

            // If options no longer valid, fall back to simple pricing.
            if (groups.length === 0) {
                showVariantMode(false);
                return;
            }

            // Keep the variant table in step with the options once it exists.
            if (!variantsWrapper.classList.contains('hidden')) {
                renderVariantRows();
            }
        }

        function cartesianProduct(groups) {
            return groups.reduce((acc, group) => {
                const combos = [];
                acc.forEach(prefix => {
                    group.values.forEach(value => {
                        combos.push({
                            ...prefix,
                            [group.name]: value
                        });
                    });
                });
                return combos;
            }, [{}]);
        }

        function showVariantMode(enabled) {
            variantsWrapper.classList.toggle('hidden', !enabled);
            basePricingFields.classList.toggle('hidden', enabled);
            pricingHint.textContent = enabled ?
                'Price and stock are now set per variant below.' :
                'Set a single price and stock count, or add options below to price each variant separately.';

            if (enabled) {
                basePrice.removeAttribute('required');
                baseStock.removeAttribute('required');
            } else {
                basePrice.setAttribute('required', 'required');
                baseStock.setAttribute('required', 'required');
                variantsBody.innerHTML = '';
            }
        }

        function renderVariantRows() {
            const groups = getCurrentOptionGroups();
            if (groups.length === 0) {
                showVariantMode(false);
                return;
            }

            const combos = cartesianProduct(groups);
            variantsBody.innerHTML = '';

            combos.forEach((combo, i) => {
                const comboKey = JSON.stringify(combo);
                const saved = savedVariantData.get(comboKey) || {};
                const label = Object.entries(combo).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join(', ');

                const hiddenInputs = Object.entries(combo)
                    .map(([name, value]) =>
                        `<input type="hidden" name="variants[${i}][options][${esc(name)}]" value="${esc(value)}">`)
                    .join('');

                const row = document.createElement('tr');
                row.innerHTML = `
                    <td class="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-charcoal">
                        ${esc(label)}
                        ${hiddenInputs}
                    </td>
                    <td class="px-4 py-2">
                        <input type="number" name="variants[${i}][price]" value="${esc(saved.price ?? '')}" min="0" step="0.01" required
                            class="variant-price-input w-28 rounded-lg border border-light-gray px-2 py-1.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-primary focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary/10">
                    </td>
                    <td class="px-4 py-2">
                        <input type="number" name="variants[${i}][stock_quantity]" value="${esc(saved.stock ?? '')}" min="0" step="1" required
                            class="variant-stock-input w-24 rounded-lg border border-light-gray px-2 py-1.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-primary focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary/10">
                    </td>
                    <td class="px-4 py-2">
                        <input type="text" name="variants[${i}][sku]" value="${esc(saved.sku ?? '')}" maxlength="100" placeholder="Optional"
                            class="variant-sku-input w-28 rounded-lg border border-light-gray px-2 py-1.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-primary focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary/10">
                    </td>
                    <td class="px-4 py-2">
                        <input type="number" name="variants[${i}][weight_kg]" value="${esc(saved.weight ?? '')}" min="0" step="0.001" placeholder="Default"
                            class="variant-weight-input w-24 rounded-lg border border-light-gray px-2 py-1.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-primary focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary/10">
                    </td>
                    <td class="px-4 py-2">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <img alt="" class="variant-image-preview hidden h-9 w-9 shrink-0 rounded-lg border border-light-gray object-cover">
                            <div class="min-w-0">
                                <input type="file" name="variants[${i}][image]" accept=".jpg,.jpeg,.png,.webp"
                                    class="variant-image-input block w-36 text-[11px] text-charcoal/60 file:mr-2 file:rounded-md file:border-0 file:bg-primary/10 file:px-2 file:py-1 file:text-[10px] file:font-semibold file:text-primary">
                                <button type="button" class="variant-image-clear mt-1 hidden text-[10px] font-semibold text-sale-red hover:underline">Remove image</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </td>
                `;

                row.querySelector('.variant-price-input').addEventListener('input', e =>
                    saveVariantField(comboKey, 'price', e.target.value));
                row.querySelector('.variant-stock-input').addEventListener('input', e =>
                    saveVariantField(comboKey, 'stock', e.target.value));
                row.querySelector('.variant-sku-input').addEventListener('input', e =>
                    saveVariantField(comboKey, 'sku', e.target.value));
                row.querySelector('.variant-weight-input').addEventListener('input', e =>
                    saveVariantField(comboKey, 'weight', e.target.value));

                const imageInput = row.querySelector('.variant-image-input');
                const imagePreview = row.querySelector('.variant-image-preview');
                const imageClear = row.querySelector('.variant-image-clear');
                let previewUrl = null;

                const clearPreview = () => {
                    if (previewUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(previewUrl);
                    previewUrl = null;
                    imagePreview.src = '';
                    imagePreview.classList.add('hidden');
                    imageClear.classList.add('hidden');
                };

                imageInput.addEventListener('change', () => {
                    clearPreview();
                    const file = imageInput.files?.[0];
                    if (!file) return;

                    previewUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
                    imagePreview.src = previewUrl;
                    imagePreview.classList.remove('hidden');
                    imageClear.classList.remove('hidden');
                });

                imageClear.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    imageInput.value = '';
                    clearPreview();
                });

                variantsBody.appendChild(row);
            });

            showVariantMode(true);
        }

        function saveVariantField(comboKey, field, value) {
            const existing = savedVariantData.get(comboKey) || {};
            existing[field] = value;
            savedVariantData.set(comboKey, existing);
        }

        addOptionBtn.addEventListener('click', () => addOptionRow());
        generateVariantsBtn.addEventListener('click', renderVariantRows);

        // Before submit, if using variants, fill the (hidden) base price/stock
        // so nothing blocks submission — the backend recalculates them anyway.
        document.getElementById('productForm').addEventListener('submit', function(event) {
            // Publishing may use general photos or per-variant photos, but
            // it needs at least one of the two. Drafts are always allowed.
            if (event.submitter?.value === 'active') {
                const hasVariantImage = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.variant-image-input'))
                    .some(input => input.files?.length > 0);

                if (!selectedImages.length && !hasVariantImage) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    imageRequirementNote.classList.remove('hidden');
                    imagesInput.closest('section')?.scrollIntoView({
                        behavior: 'smooth',
                        block: 'center'
                    });
                    return;
                }
            }

            imageRequirementNote.classList.add('hidden');

            if (!variantsWrapper.classList.contains('hidden')) {
                const prices = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.variant-price-input'))
                    .map(el => parseFloat(el.value)).filter(v => !isNaN(v));
                const stocks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.variant-stock-input'))
                    .map(el => parseInt(el.value)).filter(v => !isNaN(v));

                basePrice.value = prices.length ? Math.min(...prices) : 0;
                baseStock.value = stocks.length ? stocks.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) : 0;
            }
        });

        // ---------------------------------------------------
        // RESTORE after a server-side validation error
        // ---------------------------------------------------
        (function restoreOldInput() {
            const oldOptions = Object.values(@json(old('options', [])) || {});
            const oldVariants = Object.values(@json(old('variants', [])) || {});

            if (!oldOptions.length) return;

            oldOptions.forEach(o => addOptionRow(o.name ?? '', o.values ?? ''));

            oldVariants.forEach(v => {
                if (!v.options) return;
                savedVariantData.set(JSON.stringify(v.options), {
                    price: v.price ?? '',
                    stock: v.stock_quantity ?? '',
                    sku: v.sku ?? '',
                    weight: v.weight_kg ?? '',
                });
            });

            if (oldVariants.length) renderVariantRows();
        })();

        // ---------------------------------------------------
        // IMAGE PREVIEW
        // ---------------------------------------------------
        const imagesInput = document.getElementById('images');
        const previewGrid = document.getElementById('imagePreviewGrid');
        const imageLimitNote = document.getElementById('imageLimitNote');
        const imageRequirementNote = document.getElementById('imageRequirementNote');
        const maxImages = 5;
        let selectedImages = [];

        /** The file input is read-only, so rebuild its FileList from our own list. */
        function syncImagesInput() {
            const transfer = new DataTransfer();

            selectedImages.forEach(file => transfer.items.add(file));

            imagesInput.files = transfer.files;
        }

        function renderImagePreviews() {
            previewGrid.innerHTML = '';

            selectedImages.forEach((file, index) => {
                const previewUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
                const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                wrapper.className =
                    'relative aspect-square overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-light-gray';
                wrapper.innerHTML = `
                    <img alt="" class="h-full w-full object-cover">
                    ${index === 0 ? '<span class="absolute left-1 top-1 rounded bg-primary px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold text-white">Main</span>' : ''}
                    <div class="absolute inset-x-1 bottom-1 flex gap-1">
                        <label
                            class="flex-1 cursor-pointer rounded bg-white/90 px-1 py-1 text-center text-[10px] font-semibold text-charcoal transition hover:bg-white">
                            Replace
                            <input type="file" data-replace accept=".jpg,.jpeg,.png,.webp" class="sr-only">
                        </label>
                        <button type="button" data-remove
                            class="flex-1 rounded bg-charcoal/80 px-1 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-sale-red">
                            Remove
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `;

                const preview = wrapper.querySelector('img');
                preview.src = previewUrl;
                preview.addEventListener('load', () => URL.revokeObjectURL(previewUrl), { once: true });

                wrapper.querySelector('[data-replace]').addEventListener('change', function() {
                    const replacement = this.files?.[0];
                    if (!replacement) return;

                    selectedImages[index] = replacement;
                    imageLimitNote.classList.add('hidden');
                    syncImagesInput();
                    renderImagePreviews();
                });

                wrapper.querySelector('[data-remove]').addEventListener('click', () => {
                    selectedImages.splice(index, 1);
                    imageLimitNote.classList.add('hidden');
                    syncImagesInput();
                    renderImagePreviews();
                });

                previewGrid.appendChild(wrapper);
            });
        }

        imagesInput.addEventListener('change', function() {
            const incoming = Array.from(this.files);
            const room = maxImages - selectedImages.length;

            incoming.slice(0, Math.max(room, 0)).forEach(file => {
                const alreadySelected = selectedImages.some(existing =>
                    existing.name === file.name &&
                    existing.size === file.size &&
                    existing.lastModified === file.lastModified
                );

                if (!alreadySelected) {
                    selectedImages.push(file);
                }
            });

            imageLimitNote.classList.toggle('hidden', incoming.length <= room);

            syncImagesInput();
            renderImagePreviews();
        });
    </script>

</body>

</html>

    <script>
        (function() {
            const base = {{ (float) $product->price }};
            const MAX_PERCENT = {{ \App\Models\ProductDiscount::MAX_PERCENT }};
            const typeEl = document.getElementById('type');
            const valueEl = document.getElementById('value');
            const label = document.getElementById('valueLabel');
            const price = document.getElementById('previewPrice');
            const was = document.getElementById('previewWas');
            const badge = document.getElementById('previewBadge');
            const peso = n => '₱' + n.toLocaleString('en-PH', {
                minimumFractionDigits: 2,
                maximumFractionDigits: 2
            });

            function refresh() {
                const isPercent = typeEl.value === 'percent';
                const v = parseFloat(valueEl.value);

                label.textContent = isPercent ? 'Percent off *' : 'Amount off (₱) *';
                valueEl.max = isPercent ? MAX_PERCENT : Math.max(base - 0.01, 0.01);

                const discounted = isNaN(v) ? base : (isPercent ? base * (1 - v / 100) : base - v);
                const valid = !isNaN(v) && v > 0 && discounted > 0 && discounted < base;

                if (valid) {
                    price.textContent = peso(Math.round(discounted * 100) / 100);
                    was.textContent = peso(base);
                    badge.textContent = '-' + Math.round((1 - discounted / base) * 100) + '% OFF';
                    was.classList.remove('hidden');
                    badge.classList.remove('hidden');
                } else {
                    price.textContent = peso(base);
                    was.classList.add('hidden');
                    badge.classList.add('hidden');
                }
            }

            typeEl.addEventListener('change', refresh);
            valueEl.addEventListener('input', refresh);
            refresh();
        })();
    </script>

</body>

</html>

                                    </thead>
                                    {{-- Divider: on md+ every cell of every row except the last gets a bottom line.
                                         Applied on cells so it works with any border-collapse mode.
                                         Mobile rows are already separate bordered cards. --}}

                        @endif

                        @php

                            $hasVariants = $product->hasVariants();
                            $lowVariants = $product->lowStockVariantCount($lowStock);

                                        @class([
                                            'ml-1 text-xs text-charcoal/40 line-through',
                                            'hidden' => !$product->was_price,
                                        ])>₱{{ number_format($product->price, 2) }}</span>
                                    @if ($product->liveDiscount)
                                        <span
                                            class="ml-1 rounded bg-sale-red/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-sale-red">{{ $product->liveDiscount->label }}</span>
                                    @endif
                                </div>
                            </td>
                            <td data-label="Stock" class="{{ $labelCell }}">
                                <div class="text-right md:text-left">
                                    <span data-field="stock"
                                        @class(['stock-cell', 'is-low' => $product->isLowStock($lowStock)])>{{ $product->stock_quantity }}</span>
                                    <span data-field="stock-note" @class(['stock-note', 'hidden' => $lowVariants === 0])>{{ $lowVariants }}
                                        {{ $lowVariants === 1 ? 'variant' : 'variants' }} low</span>
                                </div>
                            </td>
                            <td data-label="Status" class="{{ $labelCell }}">
                                <span data-badge="status"
                                    class="status-badge badge--{{ $variant }}">{{ $product->status }}</span>
                            </td>
                            <td class="block pt-1 md:table-cell md:pt-0 md:text-right">
                                <div class="flex items-center gap-2 md:justify-end">
                                    <a href="{{ route('seller.products.discount.edit', $product) }}"
                                        class="btn-soft flex-1 text-center !px-3.5 !py-2 !text-xs md:flex-none">Discount</a>
                                    <button type="button" @click="openProduct({{ $product->id }})"
                                        aria-label="Manage {{ $product->name }}"
                                        class="btn-primary flex-1 !px-3.5 !py-2 !text-xs !font-semibold md:flex-none">
                                        Manage
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </td>
                        </tr>

                        @if ($loop->last)
                            </tbody>
                            </table>
                </div>
                @endif
            @empty
                <div class="flex h-64 flex-col items-center justify-center px-4 text-center">
                    <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-charcoal">No products found</p>
                    <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-charcoal/50">Try adjusting your search or filters, or add
                        your first product.</p>
                </div>
                @endforelse

        </div>

        <div class="pagination-frost overflow-x-auto">{{ $products->withQueryString()->links() }}</div>

        </main>

    </div>

    </div>


    <!-- =====================================================
         MANAGE PRODUCT MODAL (bottom sheet on mobile, centered dialog from sm)
    ====================================================== -->
    <div x-show="modalOpen" x-cloak x-transition.opacity @click.self="closeModal()"
        @keydown.escape.window="modalOpen && closeModal()"
        class="fixed inset-0 z-[70] flex items-end justify-center bg-charcoal/50 backdrop-blur-sm sm:items-center sm:p-4">

                                                    :aria-label="'Show ' + variantLabel(v) + ' to buyers'">
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                    </template>
                                </tbody>
                            </table>
                        </div>

                        <p class="mt-2 text-xs text-charcoal/45">Inactive variants are hidden from buyers. They are
                            never deleted, so past orders keep their history. Price and stock for the whole product are

                    </div>

                        <button type="button" @click="setStatus('archived')"
                            :disabled="saving || status === 'archived'" class="btn-soft btn-soft--danger flex-1">

                        </select>

                        @if ($rating || $productId || $status)
                            <a href="{{ route('seller.reviews.index') }}"
                                class="flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-white/70 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-charcoal/60 transition hover:text-primary">
                                Clear filters
                            </a>
                        @endif
                    </form>
                </div>

                {{-- REVIEW LIST --}}
                @if ($reviews->isEmpty())
                    <div class="frost-panel flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl p-14 text-center">
                        <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-charcoal/60">
                            {{ $stats['count'] === 0 ? 'No reviews yet' : 'No reviews match these filters' }}
                        </p>
                        <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-charcoal/40">
                            {{ $stats['count'] === 0
                                ? 'Reviews appear here once buyers confirm their orders and rate your products.'
                                : 'Try clearing a filter to see more reviews.' }}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                @else
                    <div class="space-y-4">
                        @foreach ($reviews as $review)
                            @php
                                $bag = 'reply_' . $review['id'];
                                $hasError = $errors->hasBag($bag);
                                $replyValue = old('review_id') == $review['id'] ? old('reply') : $review['reply'];

                                $isGoodReview = $review['rating'] >= 4;
                                $templates = collect($replyTemplates[$isGoodReview ? 'positive' : 'negative'])
                                    ->map(
                                        fn($t) => [
                                            'label' => $t['label'],
                                            'text' => str_replace(
                                                ['{product}', '{shop}'],
                                                [$review['product'], $sellerProfile->business_name ?? 'our shop'],
                                                $t['text'],
                                            ),
                                        ],
                                    )
                                    ->values();
                            @endphp

                            <div class="frost-panel rounded-2xl p-5">

                                <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                                    <div class="min-w-0">
                                        <p class="text-sm font-semibold text-charcoal">{{ $review['buyer'] }}</p>
                                        <p class="mt-0.5 text-sm leading-none">
                                            <span
                                                class="text-amber-400">{{ str_repeat('★', $review['rating']) }}</span><span
                                                class="text-charcoal/15">{{ str_repeat('★', 5 - $review['rating']) }}</span>
                                        </p>
                                    </div>

                                    <div class="flex shrink-0 flex-col items-end gap-1">
                                        <p class="text-xs text-charcoal/40">
                                            {{ $review['created_at']->format('M d, Y · g:i A') }}</p>
                                        @unless ($review['reply'])
                                            <span
                                                class="rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-600">
                                                Needs reply
                                            </span>
                                        @endunless
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <p class="mt-2 text-xs text-charcoal/50">
                                    {{ $review['product'] }} ·
                                    <a href="{{ route('seller.orders.show', $review['order_id']) }}"
                                        class="font-semibold text-primary hover:underline">Order
                                        #{{ $review['order_id'] }}</a>
                                </p>

                                @if (filled($review['comment']))
                                    <p class="mt-3 whitespace-pre-line text-sm text-charcoal/80">
                                        {{ $review['comment'] }}</p>
                                @else
                                    <p class="mt-3 text-sm italic text-charcoal/35">Rated without a written review.</p>
                                @endif

                                @if ($review['media']->isNotEmpty())
                                    <div class="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                                        @foreach ($review['media'] as $media)
                                            @if ($media['is_video'])
                                                <video src="{{ $media['url'] }}" controls preload="metadata"
                                                    class="h-24 w-32 rounded-lg bg-charcoal/10 object-cover"></video>
                                            @else
                                                <a href="{{ $media['url'] }}" target="_blank" rel="noopener"
                                                    class="block h-24 w-24 overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-charcoal/10">
                                                    <img src="{{ $media['url'] }}" alt="Review photo" loading="lazy"
                                                        class="h-full w-full object-cover">
                                                </a>
                                            @endif
                                        @endforeach
                                    </div>
                                @endif

                                {{-- SELLER REPLY --}}
                                <div x-data="{
                                    editing: {{ $hasError ? 'true' : 'false' }},
                                    reply: {{ \Illuminate\Support\Js::from($replyValue ?? '') }},
                                    templates: {{ \Illuminate\Support\Js::from($templates) }},
                                    pick(text) {
                                        this.reply = text;
                                        this.$nextTick(() => this.$refs.reply.focus());
                                    }
                                }" class="mt-4 border-t border-white/60 pt-4">

                                    @if ($review['reply'])
                                        <div x-show="!editing" class="rounded-xl bg-primary/5 px-4 py-3">
                                            <p class="text-xs font-bold text-primary">
                                                Your reply · {{ $review['replied_at']?->diffForHumans() }}
                                            </p>
                                            <p class="mt-1 whitespace-pre-line text-sm text-charcoal/80">
                                                {{ $review['reply'] }}</p>

                                            <div class="mt-2 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs font-semibold">
                                                <button type="button" @click="editing = true"
                                                    class="text-primary hover:underline">Edit</button>

                                                <form method="POST"
                                                    action="{{ route('seller.reviews.reply.destroy', $review['id']) }}"
                                                    onsubmit="return confirm('Delete your reply to this review?');">
                                                    @csrf
                                                    @method('DELETE')
                                                    <button type="submit"
                                                        class="text-sale-red hover:underline">Delete</button>
                                                </form>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    @else
                                        <button type="button" x-show="!editing" @click="editing = true"
                                            class="rounded-lg border border-primary px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-primary transition hover:bg-primary/5">
                                            Reply
                                        </button>
                                    @endif

                                    <form method="POST" action="{{ route('seller.reviews.reply', $review['id']) }}"
                                        x-show="editing" style="display: none;" class="space-y-2">
                                        @csrf
                                        <input type="hidden" name="review_id" value="{{ $review['id'] }}">

                                        {{-- QUICK REPLIES: click to fill the box, then edit freely or write your own --}}
                                        <div>
                                            <p
                                                class="mb-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-charcoal/45">
                                                {{ $isGoodReview ? 'Quick replies for good reviews' : 'Quick replies for low ratings' }}
                                            </p>
                                            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                                                <template x-for="t in templates" :key="t.label">
                                                    <button type="button" @click="pick(t.text)" x-text="t.label"
                                                        :class="reply === t.text ? 'border-primary bg-primary text-white' :
                                                            'border-primary/30 bg-white/70 text-primary hover:bg-primary/5'"
                                                        class="rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold transition">
                                                    </button>
                                                </template>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>

                                        <textarea name="reply" rows="3" maxlength="500" required x-ref="reply" x-model="reply"
                                            placeholder="Pick a quick reply above, or write your own…"
                                            class="w-full resize-none rounded-xl border border-white/70 bg-white/70 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-primary focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary/10">{{ $replyValue }}</textarea>

                                        @if ($hasError)
                                            <p class="text-xs font-semibold text-sale-red">
                                                {{ $errors->getBag($bag)->first('reply') }}
                                            </p>
                                        @endif

                                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                                            <p class="text-[11px] text-charcoal/40">
                                                <span x-text="reply.length">0</span>/500 · Buyers can see your reply.
                                                <button type="button" x-show="reply.length" @click="reply = ''"
                                                    class="ml-1 font-semibold text-primary hover:underline">Clear</button>
                                            </p>

                                            <div class="flex gap-2">
                                                <button type="button" @click="editing = false"
                                                    class="rounded-lg border border-charcoal/15 px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-charcoal/60 transition hover:text-charcoal">
                                                    Cancel
                                                </button>
                                                <button type="submit"
                                                    class="rounded-lg bg-primary px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-sky-blue">
                                                    {{ $review['reply'] ? 'Save reply' : 'Post reply' }}
                                                </button>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </form>
                                </div>

                            </div>
                        @endforeach
                    </div>

                    <div>{{ $reviews->onEachSide(1)->links() }}</div>
                @endif

            </main>

        </div>

    </div>

</body>

</html>

                        </div>

                </div>

                <script>
                    function generateCode() {
                        fetch('{{ route('seller.vouchers.generate-code') }}', {
                                method: 'POST',
                                headers: {
                                    'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').getAttribute(
                                        'content'),
                                    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                                }
                            })
                            .then(res => res.json())
                            .then(data => {
                                document.getElementById('voucherCode').value = data.code;
                            });
                    }
                </script>
            </main>

                                        {{ old('discount_type', $shopVoucher->discount_type) === 'percentage' ? 'selected' : '' }}>

                                @enderror

                            </div>

                                    (₱)</label>

                                @enderror
                            </div>

                            <div>
                                <label class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-semibold text-charcoal">Max Discount Amount
                                    (₱)</label>
                                <input type="number" name="max_discount_amount" step="0.01" min="0"
                                    value="{{ old('max_discount_amount', $shopVoucher->max_discount_amount) }}"
                                    class="w-full rounded-lg border border-light-gray px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-primary">
                                <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-charcoal/50">For percentage discounts only</p>
                                @error('max_discount_amount')
                                    <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-sale-red">{{ $message }}</p>
                                @enderror
                            </div>

                            <div class="mb-4">
                                <label class="mb-1 block text-sm font-semibold" for="claim_limit">Claim limit (optional)</label>
                                <input id="claim_limit" type="number" name="claim_limit" min="1" value="{{ old('claim_limit', $shopVoucher->claim_limit) }}" class="w-full rounded border border-line px-3 py-2 text-sm">
                                <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-charcoal/60">Maximum buyers who can claim. Leave empty for unlimited. Each claim is usable once.</p>
                                @error('claim_limit') <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-sale-red">{{ $message }}</p> @enderror
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label class="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-semibold text-charcoal">Total Quantity</label>
                                <input type="number" name="total_quantity" min="1"
                                    value="{{ old('total_quantity', $shopVoucher->total_quantity) }}"
                                    class="w-full rounded-lg border border-light-gray px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-primary">

FILE: tests/Feature/NavigationTest.php
================================================================================
<?php

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Blade;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

beforeEach(function () {
    $this->withoutVite();
    (require database_path('migrations/0001_01_01_000000_create_users_table.php'))->up();
    Schema::table('users', function (Blueprint $table) {
        $table->string('role')->default('buyer');
    });
    Schema::create('order_returns', function (Blueprint $table) {
        $table->id();
        $table->unsignedBigInteger('seller_id')->nullable();
        $table->string('status');
    });
    Schema::create('wishlist_items', function (Blueprint $table) {
        $table->id();
        $table->unsignedBigInteger('user_id');
        $table->unsignedBigInteger('product_id');
    });
    Schema::create('buyer_addresses', function (Blueprint $table) {
        $table->id();
        $table->unsignedBigInteger('user_id');
        $table->boolean('is_default');
        $table->softDeletes();
    });
    (require database_path('migrations/2026_10_22_161655_create_notifications_table.php'))->up();
});

it('renders every role through one shared shell with unique IDs and working route names', function (string $role, string $component, string $expected) {
    $this->actingAs(User::factory()->create(['role' => $role]));
    $html = Blade::render('<'.$component.' />', ['cartCount' => 0]);
    expect($html)->toContain('navigation-toggle.window', 'nav-shell', $expected, 'aria-controls=', 'method="POST"', 'name="_token"');
    expect($html)->not->toContain('sidebarNav', 'data-sidebar-toggle', '$store.sidebar');
    preg_match_all('/(?<![:\w-])id="([^"\s]+)"/', $html, $ids);
    expect(count($ids[1]))->toBe(count(array_unique($ids[1])));
})->with([
    ['seller', 'x-navbars.seller', 'Prepare &amp; Ship Orders'],
    ['logistics', 'x-navbars.logistics', 'Session History'],
    ['super_admin', 'x-navbars.admin', 'Policies'],
]);

it('keeps buyer location profile cart wishlist and notification controls reachable', function () {
    $this->actingAs(User::factory()->create(['role' => 'buyer']));
    $html = Blade::render('<x-Buyer.header :cart-count="0" :cart-preview-items="collect()" />');
    expect($html)->toContain('buyer-navigation', 'buyerSearchInput', 'buyerCartBadge', 'buyer-mobile-profile', 'buyer-desktop-profile', 'data-buyer-header-location', 'buyer-notification-feed', 'Vouchers', 'Track My Order');
    preg_match_all('/(?<![:\w-])id="([^"\s]+)"/', $html, $ids);
    expect(count($ids[1]))->toBe(count(array_unique($ids[1])));
});

it('renders guest and auth navigation with links to the landing sections', function () {
    $html = Blade::render('<x-nav.guest />');
    expect($html)->toContain('guest-navigation', 'guest-explore', route('home').'#categories', route('login'), route('register'));
    $this->get(route('login'))->assertOk()->assertSee('guest-navigation', false);
});

it('highlights only the matching seller order tab', function () {
    $this->actingAs(User::factory()->create(['role' => 'seller']));
    $request = Request::create('/seller/orders?status=prepare');
    $request->setRouteResolver(fn () => app('router')->getRoutes()->getByName('seller.orders.index'));
    app()->instance('request', $request);
    $html = Blade::render('<x-navbars.seller />');
    expect(substr_count($html, 'aria-current="page"'))->toBe(1);
    expect($html)->toContain('Prepare &amp; Ship Orders');
});

it('keeps a single Alpine bootstrap and removes the disconnected legacy handlers', function () {
    $app = file_get_contents(resource_path('js/app.js'));
    expect(substr_count($app, 'Alpine.start()'))->toBe(1);
    expect($app)->not->toContain("Alpine.store('sidebar'");
    expect(is_file(resource_path('js/shared/navbar.js')))->toBeFalse();
});

FILE: tests/Unit/NavigationState.test.js
================================================================================
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { navbar } from '../../resources/js/shared/navigation.js';

function state(width = 375) {
    const events = [];
    globalThis.window = { innerWidth: width, dispatchEvent: event => events.push(event.detail) };
    globalThis.document = { documentElement: { style: { overflow: 'clip' } }, body: { style: { overflow: 'auto' } }, activeElement: { focus() {} } };
    globalThis.localStorage = { getItem: () => null, setItem() {} };
    const nav = navbar({ id: 'test-navigation' });
    nav.$refs = { panel: { querySelector: () => ({ focus() {} }) } };
    nav.$nextTick = callback => callback();
    nav.$watch = (key, callback) => {
        let value = nav[key];
        Object.defineProperty(nav, key, { get: () => value, set: next => { value = next; callback(next); } });
    };
    nav.init();
    return { nav, events };
}

for (const width of [320, 375, 414, 768, 844]) {
    test(`mobile navigation state, scroll lock and closing at ${width}px`, () => {
        const { nav } = state(width);
        let focused = false;
        nav.toggle({ focus: () => { focused = true; } });
        assert.equal(nav.open, true);
        assert.equal(document.body.style.overflow, 'hidden');
        nav.escape();
        assert.equal(nav.open, false);
        assert.equal(document.body.style.overflow, 'auto');
        assert.equal(document.documentElement.style.overflow, 'clip');
        assert.equal(focused, true);
        nav.destroy();
    });
}

test('desktop resize closes the drawer and restores scrolling', () => {
    const { nav } = state();
    nav.toggle(); window.innerWidth = 1024; nav.resize();
    assert.equal(nav.open, false); assert.equal(nav.desktop, true);
    assert.equal(document.body.style.overflow, 'auto');
    nav.toggle(); assert.equal(nav.open, false);
    nav.destroy();
});

test('only one dropdown opens and Escape restores its trigger', () => {
    const { nav } = state(1024);
    let focused = false; const trigger = { focus: () => { focused = true; } };
    nav.dropdown('profile', trigger); nav.dropdown('cart', trigger);
    assert.equal(nav.activeDropdown, 'cart');
    nav.escape(); assert.equal(nav.activeDropdown, null); assert.equal(focused, true);
    nav.destroy();
});

test('a navigation link closes the menu and does not submit logout', () => {
    const { nav } = state(); nav.toggle();
    nav.navigate({ target: { closest: () => ({ href: '/orders' }) } });
    assert.equal(nav.open, false); assert.equal(nav.logoutOpen, false);
    nav.destroy();
});

test('logout requires confirmation and cleans up the scroll lock', () => {
    const { nav } = state(); let submitted = false;
    nav.$refs.logoutDialog = { querySelector: () => ({ focus() {} }) };
    nav.requestLogout({ submit: () => { submitted = true; }, querySelector: () => ({ focus() {} }) });
    assert.equal(nav.logoutOpen, true); assert.equal(submitted, false);
    assert.equal(document.body.style.overflow, 'hidden');
    nav.confirmLogout(); assert.equal(submitted, true);
    nav.escape(); assert.equal(document.body.style.overflow, 'auto');
    nav.destroy();
});
